65. **FIXED (2026-10-06): `dedupe()` merged DIFFERENT properties on address similarity, so tens of thousands of leads vanished every full run. Future runs only; the 10/5 checkpoint being published now is not repaired.**
    **The rules that did it (real rows, fresh four-source scrape of item 63/64).** The house-number guard (`_provably_different_property`) only refuses when BOTH rows carry DIFFERENT numbers. (1) Pass 2 (fuzzy, `token_set_ratio >= 92` in one zip or county) read a county's "no number" sentinel as a number on both sides: 33 different `0 SOUTHPORT RD` lots of spartanburg_vacant (30 SPARTANBURG + 3 ROEBUCK, 33 parcels, e.g. 710284676520, 711220615817) became ONE row; `0 HIGH ST` took `0 HUGH ST` and `0 S HIGH POINT RD`. 2,038 of the merge edges were sentinel against sentinel with two valid parcels. (2) A numberless street has no number at all, so it matched every house on its road and the merged row kept the numberless address, so it went on absorbing: `BANKS TOWN RD` 974204604700000 + `67 BANKS TOWN RD` 974213621500000, `LOOKOUT RD` + `328 LOOKOUT RD` (item 64's pair), `CAMPBELL ST` x4 parcels, `US 70 HWY` + `99999 US 70 HWY`. (3) Same number, different street, both with parcels: `128 GEORGE ST` / `128 GEORGIA ST`, `209 BUTLER ST` / `209 BUNKER ST`. (4) Pass 3's canonical-street signature (`0 southport rd`, county, state) joined the ROEBUCK lots to the SPARTANBURG ones and `123 WILLIAMS ST SPARTANBURG` to `123 WILLIAMS ST WOODRUFF`; union-find then chains through any member. (5) Separate data loss in pass 1: a row refused by the guard was parked under `<key>#hn<number>`, and a second row with that number OVERWROTE it (72 rows deleted outright in the fresh replay below, 52 on the board, e.g. charlotte_open_data units of parcel 04504206, burke_storm_damage `303 SANFORD DR`).
    **Fix (240b8de9).** `src/foreclosure_scraper/dedupe.py`: `identity_conflict()` (:222) decides every merge in all three passes (:382 pass 1, :510 pass 2, :559 pass 3), against the whole group merged so far (pass 1 bucket identity, pass 2 running group, pass 3 group per union-find root), not just the row that matched. Refused: two different real house numbers (`placeholder_twins.real_house_no`, so a sentinel is not a number and `000141` is 141); two different VALID parcels (`placeholder_twins.parcel_key`: 7+ chars, not one repeated digit, not a book/page pattern, digitless garbage already rejected; a resolver-attached parcel, `placeholder_twins.resolver_parcel`, is no evidence either way; the same parcel number in two counties is two parcels, one county spelled two ways like Rutherford/Rutherfordton is one, :197); a row with no real number matching a numbered row unless the valid parcels agree; two unnumbered rows on ADDRESS evidence (fuzzy score, address key or address signature) unless the valid parcels agree. A shared case number or URL still merges as before. When an unnumbered row does merge with a numbered one (same valid parcel), the row takes the numbered address (`_merge`, :239, like `placeholder_twins.fold()`), so a sentinel twin in the fresh scrape now merges and shows `499 PATCH DR`. Pass 1 never drops a refused row (next non-conflicting `<key>\x00n` bucket). Pass 2 skips a pair whose valid parcels differ before scoring it (:480; per-row parcels at :436) and computes each normalized address once (:430): same merges, faster. `board_persist.merge_prior_board()`: a strict match is also refused across two different valid parcels (`_different_valid_parcels`, board_persist.py:244, used at :408; new stat `refused_different_parcel`); the house-number guard and the placeholder-twin fallback are unchanged. `board_dedupe_stream`'s light rows now carry the resolver flags so the streamed finder matches `dedupe()` (:190). `_provably_different_property` is kept for scripts/ingest_new_county_sources.py.
    **Measured: first dedupe, fresh scrape.** 27 scrapers run locally on 10/5-10/6 (56 source slugs incl. the arcgis layers; gaston/lincoln/transylvania vacant, nc_ptscloud, sc_ust_registry, nc_ecourts_lis_pendens, qpaybill (partial), county tax rolls, column notices, plus item 63's four), 185,178 rows after main.run()'s own filters, about 70% of the 9/22 full run's scraped rows. Old: 185,178 to 133,800 rows (337 s), 6,992 rows had absorbed 32,720 records of other properties (26,410 distinct valid parcels), 72 rows deleted. New: 174,929 rows (239 s), no group holds two valid parcels or two real numbers, 0 rows missing. 9,448 old groups split, 42,320 rows restored: 38,129 have a different parcel from the row they had been folded into, 2,083 a different permit/case number (sc_ust_registry facilities `TOLLISONS GROCERY` UST00421 vs `MOORES GRO & CAFE` UST00423, both `HWY 81`), 1 a different house number, 2,107 not provable either way (mostly numbered landslide/permit rows with no parcel that a numberless parcel row such as `FLAT CREEK RD` had absorbed). By source: gaston_vacant 14,250, lincoln_vacant 12,372, transylvania_vacant 5,511, sc_ust_registry 2,606, spartanburg_vacant 1,536, rutherford_tax 1,290, qpaybill 1,047. 1,233 groups the old rule did not form: 1,186 re-unite copies of one property the old run had folded into different neighbours, 45 hold a row the old pass 1 deleted (charlotte condo units under one parcel), 2 other. The four sources alone: 16,932 to 14,128 (old) vs 16,904 (new), 839 groups split, 2,776 rows restored, every one a different parcel (the 84 Rutherford `0 E MAIN ST` style groups have distinct 6-digit parcels).
    **Measured: board-wide (dedupe2 view).** `dedupe()` over all 223,832 published rows (light identity projection, streamed): old 209,756 rows (608 s), 415 groups with two valid parcels or two real numbers absorbed 1,247 rows (773 valid parcels), 52 rows deleted; new 210,851 rows (296 s). 576 groups split, 1,103 rows restored (1,020 live, 83 aged-only): 407 different valid parcels (`TIPTON HILL RD` + 149/154/155/163/245 TIPTON HILL RD, each its own parcel), 8 different house numbers, 128 numbered vs unnumbered without validated parcel agreement (`PINEY MOUNTAIN DR` 9658289934 vs `305 PINEY MOUNTAIN DR` no parcel), 33 both unnumbered. 39 new groups: 31 hold a row the old pass 1 deleted, 7 leading-zero numbers with one parcel (`00401 FAIRVIEW CIR` = `401 FAIRVIEW CIR`, 0568050105900), 1 other.

Fresh set as it would publish (short parcels nulled like validation.py): 135 to 149 (+14). The board-wide 161 unproven splits are not visible to selfcheck (one side has no parcel); some of them are probably one property left as two rows. Check the memory ceilings and the publish peak (14.9 GB measured at about 254K rows) before that run. The current `--publish-only` of the 10/5 checkpoint is unaffected: the resume and publish tail never call `dedupe()`.

Faster: 14 s vs 39 s (four sources), 239 s vs 337 s (fresh set), 296 s vs 608 s (board).

Rows sharing a garbage or fused parcel and the SAME house number on different streets still merge in pass 1 (`288 WHITEHOUSE DIRVE` + `288 N NC 16 BUSINESS HWY` under resolver parcel 3633940779), as before. Junk "addresses" read as house numbers (`220 Having qualified as Administrator ...`). Units of one building that share one parcel (charlotte `4528 WYNBROOK WY #47` / `#35`) still merge, as before. 66. **BUILT (2026-10-06): Per-listing verification (phase 2). Every row can now carry `raw['verification']`: a live, re-checkable verdict per claim from the claim's own source, produced by the pipeline, not a sample. The reference verifier is `tax_lien` (Buncombe NC), live on 280 properties; the VM applies the verdicts before scoring, and a refuted or stale verdict takes the signal out of the score.**
    **How it flows (the item 62 Mac -> VM pattern).** Mac: `scripts/verification_sweep.py` streams the board read-only (`board_stream.iter_board_rows()`), runs each auto-discovered verifier on the rows it applies to, merges every answer into the per-signal ledger `docs/handoff/verification/<signal>.json`, and commits and pushes ONLY those files (`HANDOFF_PUSH=0` skips git; `pull --rebase --autostash` before the push; the board lock only around the commit and rebase). VM: `main.py` `run_enrich_tail()` calls `verification.apply.apply_verification(enriched)` right before `score_board` (one wiring point, so the nightly run and the enrich-only resume both get it): no network, it attaches each matching ledger entry's latest result to the row. It never fails the run (`verification_apply.absent/empty/unreadable/failed` logged); `VERIFICATION_APPLY=0` turns it off. `verification` is `"*"` in `web_artifact.RAW_KEEP`. Not in `_SLIM_RAW`/`dashboard.js` `_LEAN_RAW` yet, so the desktop board carries it and the phone slim file does not.
    **Where everything lives.** `src/foreclosure_scraper/verification/`: `core.py` (`VerificationResult`, verdicts, `row_key()`/`row_keys()`, and the read side the scorer uses: `suppressed_scorer_signals(raw, today)`, `verdict_badges(raw)`), `registry.py` (auto-discovery + the verifier contract), `verifiers/` (one module per signal and source), `ledger.py` (`Ledger`, `is_due()`, `publish_ledgers()`), `apply.py` (the VM step), `fetch.py` (`Fetcher`: http_client's per-host throttle plus `VERIFY_HOST_MIN_INTERVAL_S`, default 1.5 s, per host and optional response capture; `ReplayFetcher` for tests). `scripts/verification_sweep.py`. Scoring hooks: `distress_score._collect` and `enrichment_lead_signals._facet_signals`.
    **The record** (`raw['verification']`, a list, one per signal; the spec's schema plus three stamps): `{signal, verdict, evidence, source, checked_at (UTC), verifier_version, verifier, expires_at, governs}`. `expires_at` = `checked_at` + the verifier's current `TTL_DAYS`; `governs` = the scorer signal names a refuted/stale verdict removes (the module's current `GOVERNS`; a record with no module, e.g. the human lane's, uses what its ledger entry stored). Verdicts, defined once in `core.py`: `confirmed` (true today), `refuted` (false, no sign it ever was true), `stale` (WAS true, no longer: a bill paid late), `unconfirmed` (checked, source could not decide), `wall` (cannot be checked by code; never queried). An expired record is ignored by the scorer and the badge, and is re-checked by the sweep.
    **Scoring uses the verdicts, minimally.** A non-expired `refuted` or `stale` verdict removes the scorer signals in its `governs` from `_collect` (signals and lane events) and from the lead-signal facets. `stale` suppresses too: a delinquency paid since is not distress today. `confirmed` changes no weight and is readable for a badge (`core.verdict_badges(raw)` -> `{"tax_lien": "confirmed"}`); `unconfirmed` and `wall` change nothing. A `"<name>:<qualifier>"` entry in GOVERNS is a partial rule implemented in BOTH readers: `recorded_debt:tax` drops the recorded_debt credit only where the debt is the tax balance (`raw.tax_owed`, or `amount_owed.source == "tax_owed"`); a judgment or an opening bid still counts.
    **The ledger.** One file per signal, one entry per property per line, sorted (`{"schema":1,"kind":"verification_ledger","signal",...,"rows":{key: entry}}`). An entry: `keys` (every `row_keys()` value), `row` (summary), `latest` (the full result, evidence trimmed by `core.compact`), `history` (the last 6 earlier answers, stamps only), `last_attempt`, `checks`, `first_checked_at`, `ttl_days`, `governs`. Rules: entries are never dropped; atomic writes; a decisive answer always becomes `latest`; an `unconfirmed`/`wall` answer never displaces a decisive verdict that is within its TTL and of the current version (a flaky page cannot erase a real verdict; a human-lane `wall` placeholder cannot either). `is_due()`: never checked; checked by another verifier or another VERSION; decisive or wall older than `TTL_DAYS`; unconfirmed older than `RETRY_DAYS`. The sweep merges with the file on disk before every save (every 10 rows and at the end), so two writers lose nothing. About 1.25 KB per property (280 entries = 351 KB); if a signal's file passes ~20 MB, shard it by county inside the same directory.
    **Row identity, and the evidence for it.** `row_key(row)` = `parcel:<STATE>:<county>:<parcel>` (normalized exactly like `Listing.dedupe_key()`'s parcel branch) when the row has a parcel, else `addr:<STATE>:<county>:<address>` (`models._normalize_addr`, NO zip, and only when the address has a real house number > 0), else `case:<STATE>:<county>:<case>`, else `row:<fingerprint>` of the as-scraped fields (never the source_url). The ledger keeps every key of the row and a row matches an entry on any key exactly one entry claims, but never an entry holding a DIFFERENT parcel. Measured with `board_stream` on the current board and the committed boards of 2026-10-01 (533d8e58) and 2026-09-21 (7c974c74): `source_url` is unique for 46% of rows (one roll URL covers every lead in the file), so it cannot identify a row. 10/1 -> 10/5 (one VM production run, rows carried forward): every key type persisted 100%. 9/21 -> 10/5 (two weeks of runs, merges and backfills): a single `dedupe_key()` string stayed unique for only 74.4% of rows; among rows matched by a unique address, 31,292 changed dedupe_key (19,928 addr -> parcel when a parcel was backfilled, 4,211 parcel -> addr, 7,153 parcel -> parcel). With these keys: the single primary key persisted for 84.8% (Buncombe tax-flagged rows 71.9%), the any-key match for 92.2% (95.7%), and only 176 keys (0.1%) pointed at two rows; the rest are rows that left the board. The house-number and parcel-conflict rules (4a22cabc) came from the live sweep: bare road names on Buncombe vacant-land rows ("OLD TRULL RD", "NC 9 HWY", "S TURKEY CREEK RD") joined up to four parcels to one entry; that ledger was rebuilt (f1943000; the old file is in git history). Two board rows of the same parcel share one entry and one verdict, which is right for a property-level fact (a parcel's taxes are paid or not, whichever source's row asks).
    **The reference verifier: `verifiers/tax_lien_buncombe.py` (v2), evolved from `validate_tax_lien_buncombe.py`.** Applies to Buncombe NC rows the validator selected: `listing_type == tax_lien`, `two_year_delinquent.is_two_year_plus`, or a surfaced `tax_aging_surfaced` with a delinquent year (3,157 rows today). PIN: 15 digits; a 10-digit base PIN gets `00000`; a zero-padded 11-14 digit one is cut back; a condo unit keeps its suffix (`9648623059C0401`, `9644953081C0D17`, both live-checked). Fetches `tax.buncombenc.gov/Parcel/Details/{pin}` (plain HTML, no auth, no CAPTCHA) and parses each billing-history card. A levy-year Y bill is delinquent once unpaid after January 5 of Y+1 (G.S. 105-360), so today the 2026 bill is not and the 2025 one is. `confirmed`: a delinquent bill with an amount due. Nothing delinquent: if the parcel's billing ends before the latest delinquent-eligible levy (a retired PIN; v2) -> `unconfirmed` (`parcel_record_ended`); otherwise it fetches the Bill Details page of the year the board claimed (`buncombe_delinquent_tax.tax_year`, `two_year_delinquent.tax_year`, `tax_aging_surfaced.tax_year`, `tax_owed.year`) or the latest delinquent-eligible levy, at most 2: paid after the deadline or interest/late fee charged -> `stale`, paid on time -> `refuted`. Fetch failure, unparseable page, unresolvable PIN -> `unconfirmed`. Evidence: URL, PIN, delinquent amount per year, total, years delinquent, the not-yet-delinquent current levy, claimed years, bills checked (paid-on date, interest), and the FINDINGS side checks: county owner vs board owner (`owner_match`) and county value vs board value (`value_ratio_board_to_county`). GOVERNS `tax_lien`, `tax_sale`, `tax_lien_chronic`, `recorded_debt:tax`. TTL 30 days, retry 7.
    **Live sweeps on this Mac, 2026-10-06 (all pushed; ~600 polite requests in total, 0 errors).** After the rebuild under v2 the ledger holds 280 Buncombe properties: confirmed 163, stale 39, refuted 47, unconfirmed 31 (28 `parcel_record_ended`; 3 unresolvable: 2 alphanumeric condo PINs that cb80a388 now resolves, re-tried after 2026-10-13, and 1 liensnc row with no parcel). By slice: the HOT/WARM head the sweep takes first (100 properties, 37 HOT + 63 WARM, almost all from the ArcGIS `buncombe_unpaid_bills` layer): confirmed 70, stale 11, refuted 2, unconfirmed 17 (HOT 37: 27 confirmed, 8 stale, 2 refuted). A COLD sample of the same order (50): 43 confirmed, 2 stale, 5 unconfirmed. `counties_nc.buncombe_delinquent_tax`, WARM, first 50 in board order: 46 confirmed. The same source, COLD (40): 3 confirmed, 20 stale, 15 refuted. `liensnc` rows typed tax_lien (40): 1 confirmed, 5 stale, 30 refuted (lien-agent filings, not tax liens). Of the confirmed, most balances are small (51 of the first 70 under $500). Board value vs the county's: median 1.73x, 225 of 267 overstated by more than 10%, 25 within 10% (FINDINGS #1 Finding B, reproduced). County owner differs from the board's on 41 of 277 (Finding C).
    **Against FINDINGS' 11.9%.** No conflict, a different slice. FINDINGS drew 60 parcels uniformly from the 1,176 rows with an unpunctuated 15-digit PIN; on today's board they are 43 `buncombe_delinquent_tax` rows (12 COLD, 31 WARM), 18 liensnc rows and 1 nc_ecourts_judgments row. The ArcGIS layer (punctuated PINs) was not in it. Where both instruments checked the same parcel (6) they agree: FINDINGS' 3 real ones are confirmed, its 3 false ones are stale (2) and refuted (1). The slices that make up most of FINDINGS' population read the same here: COLD `buncombe_delinquent_tax` + liensnc = 4 confirmed of 80 (5%). What the verifier adds is the split of the false ones: 25 of those 80 were real delinquencies since paid (`stale`), 45 never were (`refuted`). The board's HOT/WARM tiers and the ArcGIS layer are mostly real; the false positives sit in COLD `buncombe_delinquent_tax` rows and liensnc rows typed tax_lien. The sweep order is board order within a tier, not random, so these slice rates are not a board-wide rate; `--tier`/`--source` take any slice.
    **Apply + scoring demo on this Mac, no write.** 147 real board rows matched to the first 100 ledger properties (+10 unchecked controls), streamed into `Listing` objects, `apply_verification()` against the pushed ledger: attached 147 records (confirmed 104, unconfirmed 27, stale 13, refuted 3), controls untouched. `_signals_for()`: every confirmed row that scored `tax_lien` still does (100/100); every refuted/stale one that scored it no longer does (15/15). E.g. 29 Ravenwood Dr (refuted: only the 2026 levy is unpaid; 2025 paid on time): `['recorded_debt', 'storm_damage', 'tax_lien']` -> `['storm_damage']`; 401 Bowling Park Rd (stale: 2025 levy paid 2026-09-30 with $719.98 interest): `tax_lien` removed, its judgment-sourced debt kept; 28 Dode Whitaker Rd (confirmed, $7,583.76 over 2023-2025): unchanged, badge `{'tax_lien': 'confirmed'}`. `score_board` on that subset: 2 HOT -> COLD, 5 HOT -> WARM, everything confirmed unchanged.
    **The human lane now writes the ledger (item 61's preview-only output replaced).** `verification_human_lane.record_to_ledger(row, record)` merges the step-1 `wall` placeholder and the step-2 verdict into `docs/handoff/verification/<signal>.json` (probate, heir_estate, lis_pendens, divorce, sos_entity), with `LEDGER_TTL_DAYS` and `LEDGER_GOVERNS` from that module; `scripts/verify_lead_human_assisted.py` writes it by default and pushes only those files (`--no-ledger`, `--no-push`, `HANDOFF_PUSH=0`). The VM applies it like any other verdict. It still never solves a CAPTCHA and never writes the board.
    **Walls.** `verifiers/divorce_sc_wall.py`: SC Public Index restricts bulk/automated querying in its terms, so this verifier never fetches and labels every SC row with a divorce claim `wall` (GOVERNS nothing). It is the template for any walled source (`WALL = True`, `GOVERNS = ()`). NC eCourts and NC SOS stay in the human lane.
    **HOW TO ADD A VERIFIER (the steps; nothing shared to edit).**
    (1) Pick the signal and its proven script (the spec's table) and confirm the source is free, no login, no CAPTCHA, and not ToS-restricted for automated querying; if it is walled, write a `WALL = True` module that returns `wall` without touching `client` (copy `divorce_sc_wall.py`) and stop there.
    (2) Create `src/foreclosure_scraper/verification/verifiers/<signal>_<scope>.py` (a name starting with `_` is ignored). Module-level: `SIGNAL` (the ledger file name; modules may share one, their `applies()` must not overlap; the first by module name wins), `VERSION = "v1"`, `TTL_DAYS`, optional `RETRY_DAYS` (default 7), `SOURCE`, `GOVERNS`. `applies(row) -> bool`: pure, no I/O, on a board dict exactly as `board_stream` yields it (slim fields and published raw; no comps/vision/cama sidecar). `async verify(row, client, *, today=None) -> VerificationResult`: fetch only through `client.get_text(url)` / `client.get_json(url)`, build the answer with `core.result(SIGNAL, verdict, evidence, source=SOURCE, version=VERSION, verifier=<module name>)`, and return `unconfirmed` with `evidence["reason"]` on every failure path (a raise is recorded as `unconfirmed`/`verifier_error`). Put the URL and what the source said in `evidence`. Use the verdict meanings in `core.py` exactly.
    (3) `GOVERNS` lists exact scorer names: the listing-type names in `distress_score._LISTING_TYPE_SIGNAL`, the names `_collect` appends (`recorded_debt`, `bankruptcy`, `incarceration`, `probate`, `divorce`, `code_enforcement`, `vacant_structure`, ...) and the facet names in `enrichment_lead_signals._facet_signals`. A rule that removes a signal only in part needs a `"<name>:<qualifier>"` entry implemented in BOTH readers, as `recorded_debt:tax` is.
    (4) Record real responses: `HANDOFF_PUSH=0 uv run python scripts/verification_sweep.py --signal <signal> --county <county> --max-rows 5 --ledger-dir /tmp/scratch_ledger --capture-dir /tmp/cap` (each body is saved with an `index.tsv` of URLs). Gzip the pages you need into `tests/fixtures/verification/` and write `tests/test_verification_<module>.py` with `fetch.ReplayFetcher({url: body})`: one test per verdict, `applies()`, and every unconfirmed path. Run `uv run pytest -q tests/test_verification_*.py tests/test_raw_keep_covers_enrichers.py`.
    (5) `--dry-run` shows the candidates. Then a bounded live run (`--max-rows 50`), check the printed verdicts, and let it push. Nothing else changes: the registry finds the module, the ledger file appears, the VM applies it next run.
    (6) Changed the verdict logic later? Bump `VERSION`: every older entry becomes due; `--recheck-only` re-checks just those.
    **Running it.** `uv run python scripts/verification_sweep.py [--signal S] [--county C] [--tier HOT,WARM,COLD] [--source slug] [--max-rows N] [--budget-s 2700] [--recheck-only] [--dry-run] [--capture-dir D] [--ledger-dir D]`. Order: HOT -> WARM -> COLD, then never checked, then oldest check; one check per property per run (`same_property_queued`). Knobs: `VERIFY_MAX_ROWS_PER_SIGNAL` (200), `VERIFY_MAX_SECONDS` (2700), `VERIFY_HOST_MIN_INTERVAL_S` (1.5), `HANDOFF_PUSH`, `VERIFICATION_LEDGER_DIR`, `VERIFICATION_APPLY` (VM). Run lock `logs/.verification_sweep.lock`. ~0.5 req/s to one host; 50 properties take 75-110 s. NOT SCHEDULED yet: a launchd job like `sosagent` (daily, `--max-rows 200`) is the next step, owner's call. Check the VM: `grep verification_apply logs/vm-run-*.log` (`verification_apply.applied ... signals=... suppressing=N`).
    **Tests** (all pass, no network): `tests/test_verification_core.py` (result schema, verdict classes, row keys incl. parcel normalization / zip / house numbers / no source_url, the read side and expiry), `test_verification_ledger.py` (merge rules, a flaky page never erases a verdict, history bound, re-key on parcel backfill, ambiguous keys, a shared address never joins two parcels, merge never loses entries, atomic one-entry-per-line save, unreadable refused, `is_due` new/version/ttl/retry), `test_verification_registry.py` (auto-discovery on a temp package: good, shared-signal, private, broken-import and broken-contract modules; the SC wall never fetches), `test_verification_apply.py` (registry TTL/GOVERNS win, stored fallback, alt-key match, other signals kept, disabled/absent/empty/unreadable/never raises/bad row, RAW_KEEP, main.py order), `test_verification_scoring.py` (refuted/stale drop `tax_lien` and the tax debt, a judgment survives, confirmed/unconfirmed/wall/expired change nothing, `score_board`, lead-signal facets, badge), `test_verification_tax_lien_buncombe.py` (real captured pages: confirmed 28 Dode Whitaker Rd, stale 40 Boone St, refuted 3 Eastcrest Dr, record-ended 249 Main Ave S; PIN forms; delinquency dates; unconfirmed paths), `test_verification_human_lane_ledger.py`, `test_verification_sweep.py` (order, dedupe, TTL, county/tier/source filters, cap, recheck-only, end to end without touching the board, crash -> unconfirmed). `tests/test_verification_*.py`: 151 passed (112 new + the 39 human-lane cases); with `test_raw_keep_covers_enrichers.py`: 326 passed. Every test file touching the scorer, lead signals or main's tail, plus the verification, RAW_KEEP, slim and SOS hand-off tests (64 files): 1,637 passed, 9 skipped, 1 failed (`test_pipeline_fixes.py::test_scope_bypass_set_deliberately_scoped`, a SCOPE_BYPASS_SOURCES set mismatch that predates this work; item 61 lists it too).
    **Commits.** Code 465e6c1e, 0a26a5d7, 4a22cabc, cb80a388, 91d89cef, d6b68be4, c508e5b3; ledger 5b7ec94a, 1e0ec00e, f1943000 (rebuild), 8fc64687, d89cc7c8, c35487c6, 9072e026. Nothing on the VM was touched and no board was written on this Mac.
    **Next.** Schedule the sweep; the per-vendor tax adapters (nc_ptscloud, qpaybill) to take `tax_lien` beyond Buncombe; the other verifiers in the spec's table, in its order (bankruptcy_stay, code_enforcement, jail_booking, elderly_disabled, lis_pendens ROD, probate ROD); add `verification` to `_SLIM_RAW` and `dashboard.js` `_LEAN_RAW` together (`tests/test_board_slim.py` pins them equal) and draw the badge.

Outputs on the VM: `logs/withdrawn_audit_20261006.json`, `logs/twin_detail_20261006.json`, `logs/stale_only_sample_20261006.json`.
    **1. The 6,414: the hypothesis is refuted as stated, the real cause is a match miss.** merge_prior_board already cleared pulled_sale and the status on every row it MATCHED (board_persist.py finalize). These rows were not matched: 5,691 were first aged by this run's merge (`first_missed_at` 2026-10-05T02:18:47Z, the merge clock) and 723 already carried the tag on the 10/2 board (consecutive_misses 2-4). The only merge between merge_prior_board and the checkpoint is dedupe2 (main.py:1879; web_artifact's merge_duplicate_rows is not in the run), which in the 10/5 code fused the live row into the aged copy (item 64), so the row keeps the aged copy's identity and the live last_seen. 5,493 are their OWN prior copy (same source, first_seen and address/parcel); 1,545 also show another source (some are item-65 fusions, e.g. qpaybill + an sc_ust_registry case number). Why no match: validation nulls a parcel id under 7 characters (16K rows on 10/5: ptscloud 5,137, nc_county_pdf 4,015, rutherford_tax 2,078, lincoln_vacant 1,972, liensnc 727), so e.g. Catawba's tax-account rows publish with parcel None and key `url:<county PDF>` while the re-scrape keys `parcel:NC:catawba:65771`; address synthesis (main.py:1826) then gave both the same `Parcel — IKERD ZANE GREY — Catawba NC delinquent tax $1,036 owed (65771)` and dedupe2 paired them. Catawba alone is 3,737 rows; live check on 10/6: all 3,519 that carry their account number are on the county's list, all 457 hud_reac_inspection rows are in the REAC pull. 143 fannie_homepath rows are a third path: enrichment_reo_freshness found them in HomePath's live inventory, set last_seen (10/6 03:2x) and left the tag. **Under today's code this would have got worse:** the identity rule (240b8de9) refuses the synthesized-address pairing, so the replay of three real Catawba accounts gives 6 rows for 3 properties (live + aged copy), one more copy per run up to the miss limit.

Correct cutoff for this publish: after the prior board's newest stamp and at or before the oldest stamp the run brought in: **2026-10-04T19:34:31Z** (one global cutoff suffices; no per-source rule needed).

Not fixed (reported): Listing.merge() lets the prior copy win raw leaf collisions in merge_prior_board (item 64's caveat (3)).

## Blocked / not worth building (as of 2026-08-20; not re-verified on 2026-09-21)

Retained from the previous edition. Re-probe before believing a "dead" entry (`docs/MASTER_GAPS_WALLS_AND_MANUAL_LANES.md` rule 4).

- PACER: paid. CourtListener covers bankruptcy.
- Oconee parcel cache: the ArcGIS server rejected bulk export. (The 9/20 parcel-cache refresh log lists Anderson and Oconee caches as OK, so the older "Anderson, Cherokee, Union, Oconee blocked" line is superseded; Cherokee and Union are not in the refresh list.)
- Several REO sources (BofA, First Bank, Founders FCU, UCBI, RealtyBid): dead, 403, 404 or SPA-walled.
- Census ACS needs a free key (`api.census.gov/data/key_signup.html`); a tract-level geocoder fallback works without one.
- FBI UCR host was unreachable. SC SOS UCC costs $5 a search. Zillow's API is discontinued. No free MLS access exists.
- doc_ocr: the first board-wide backfill ran 9/16 (commit 3ba7337, 46 rows). The 8/20 claim of "3,388 PDFs linked, 0 parsed" is superseded.

## Register of deeds (last verified 2026-08-12; not re-verified)

Two platforms, not one.

| | The Lookup | Online Record System |
|---|---|---|
| entry | `index.php?Accept=Accept` | `NameSearch.php?Accept=Accept` |
| search | `content.php` (GET) | `NamePick.php` then `NameDisplay.php` (POST) |
| amount in index | no | yes |
| counties | clay, haywood, yancey (NC) | 8 SC |
| reader | `enrichment_rod_lookup.py` | `enrichment_rod_name_index.py` |

**Wrong-state hosts are a standing trap:** `<county>deeds.com` never states its state. `hendersondeeds.com` is Henderson County, Kentucky; `wilsondeeds.com` is Wilson County, Tennessee. Full derivation: [`ROD_PORTAL_ACCESS.md`](ROD_PORTAL_ACCESS.md).

## API details (last verified 2026-08-20; not re-verified)

- GovDeals: `POST https://maestro.lqdt1.com/search/list` with `x-api-key` and `Ocp-Apim-Subscription-Key` (both in `.env`), `businessId: "GD"` as a STRING; `isAPIFailureActive: true` is normal; real property is rare on that site. Akamai-walled unless `curl_cffi` impersonates Chrome.
- HomePath: `GET https://homepath.fanniemae.com/cfl/property-inventory/search?state=NC&page=1&pageSize=5`.
- NCPTS Cloud: `https://lrcpwa.ncptscloud.com/api/SimpleParcelSearch?query={q}&pageIndex=0&pageSize=10` with an `X-Tenant: {CountyName}` header (17 NC counties).
- Listing model: `street_address` not `address`, `source_url` not `url`, `ListingType.TAX_SALE`, extras go in the `raw` dict. Registry slugs carry different suffixes (`chester_delinquent_tax`); the registry returns classes, instantiate before calling.

| doc | what |
|---|---|
| `OPERATIONS.md` | how the engine runs, the lock, the job log, publishing, recovery, repo size |
| `RESTORE.md` | restoring a bad board and the Mac itself |
| `ops_fixes_2026-09-21.md` | the operations fixes, patches to apply, what remains |
| `AUDIT_2026-09-21.md`, `audit_operations_2026-09-21.md`, `audit_signal_logic_2026-09-21.md` | the audits |
| `SOURCE_REGISTER.md` | every source with URL, gate, cost, cadence (regenerated 2026-08-20; 205 scrapers then, 209 logged on 8/28) — **generated, not hand-written**; after adding a scraper run `.venv/bin/python scripts/gen_source_register.py` (touches the board read-only) or `tests/test_source_docs_current.py` will fail on the next full-suite run. `.git/hooks/pre-commit` is still size-gate only (`scripts/git_size_gate.sh`) — it does not run pytest, and drift like the `counties_sc.dorchester_billtrax_delinquent_tax` register-entry miss (shipped in 4f2c3186) can still land in a single commit. But as of 2026-09-30, `.github/workflows/tests.yml` runs the full suite on every push to `main` and every pull request, so it now surfaces within minutes on GitHub instead of waiting for someone to run pytest by hand — see the correction below. |
| `COUNTY_SYSTEMS_REGISTRY.md` | 146 counties x 4 systems |
| `ROD_PORTAL_ACCESS.md` | both recorder platforms, request recipes |
| `MASTER_GAPS_WALLS_AND_MANUAL_LANES.md` | what cannot be done and why (see its 2026-09-21 correction block) |
| `path_to_100.md`, `gap_ledger.md` | costed blueprint; ledger |

## Commands

```bash
cd ~/foreclosure-scraper
.venv/bin/python -m pytest -q -p no:cacheprovider tests/test_<file>.py     # targeted; the suite is large
.venv/bin/python scripts/board_selfcheck.py                                # invariants, exit 1 on breach
.venv/bin/python scripts/board_manifest.py --verify
scripts/with_board_lock.sh <name> -- uv run python scripts/<writer>.py
scripts/restore_board.sh --list 10
/usr/bin/python3 scripts/job_watch.py --report
```

Never run `regenerate_dashboard.py` to fix valuations: it is a network re-enrichment of many hours. `scripts/recompute_valuation.py` does it offline.
